# Skills · L · part 22

871 entries, least-squares-regression to leeway-ui-energy-thermal-efficiency. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **least-squares-regression**: Use when you must fit a straight line to paired measurements by ordinary least squares: compute the slope and intercept of the best fit, the residual standard deviation, and the coefficient of determination, and predict the response at a new input. Produces the fit coefficients, the goodness of fit, and the residual scatter that gate whether the linear model is adequate for the analysis. Trigger: least squares, linear regression, best fit line, slope, intercept, residual standard deviation, coefficient of determination, r squared, prediction. ([ashfordeOU/aero-agent-skills](https://github.com/ashfordeOU/aero-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cross-cutting/numerics/least-squares-regression) · ★8)
- **least-to-most**: Decompose into easier sub-problems and solve in order. USE FOR complex multi-hop questions. DO NOT USE for atomic questions. ([PMCRO-AI-Agent-Company/AgentSkills.Template](https://github.com/PMCRO-AI-Agent-Company/AgentSkills.Template/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/reasoning/least-to-most))
- **least-to-most**: For a complex problem, first decompose it into an ordered list of simpler subproblems, then solve them in sequence, feeding each answer into the next, instead of one-shotting the whole thing. Use on multi-step tasks, hard algorithmic problems, or anything that fails when attempted whole. Trigger with /least-to-most or "break this down", "decompose first", "solve step by step in order". ([Zavelinski/least-to-most](https://github.com/Zavelinski/least-to-most/tree/HEAD/skills/least-to-most))
- **least-to-most-trace**: Use this skill when the user invokes /least-to-most-trace or asks for a default public reasoning trace using Least-to-Most Prompting. The skill writes a durable scratchpad to root memory/{question_name}.md and uses Least-to-Most Prompting as the actual structure of the analysis. Treat the scale as a rough effort target rather than a fixed quota: around 100 numbered lines or roughly 2,000 to 3,500 tokens of public scratchpad detail. Use this skill when the user wants the answer shaped by progressive subproblem solving from easiest to hardest with solution transfer instead of a generic response. ([cerredz/Vidbyte-Skills](https://github.com/cerredz/Vidbyte-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/least-to-most-trace))
- **least_squares_mask_tuning**: Tune surviving pruning mask coefficients with damped least squares for activation reconstruction. ([VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill](https://github.com/VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill/tree/HEAD/skills/task-oriented/PaperBench/skills/adaptive-pruning/skill/retraining_free_pruning/least_squares_mask_tuning) · ★357)

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/leave-one-genome-out-holdout-validation))
- **leave-one-habitat-out-classification-benchmark**: Use when you need to measure how much bespoke (source-environment) taxonomic weights matter, by benchmarking mis-matched or 'deliberately incorrect' habitat weights against uniform weights across habitat classes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leave-one-out-cell-type-generalization-evaluation))

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/leave-one-out-classifier-validation))
- **leave-one-out-cross-validation**: Use when benchmarking cross-species regulatory-element mapping accuracy with matched orthologous functional genomic datasets (e.g. 67 matched human-mouse ENCODE ChIP-seq/DNase-seq sets), where each dataset must be held out in turn. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/leave-one-out-cross-validation))
- **leave-one-out-cross-validation**: Use when comparing cancer-vs-normal classification accuracy of alternative gene sets (e.g., Wx-14 vs Peng-14 UGCBs) on a small-to-moderate TCGA expression matrix, where every sample must serve as a validation case to estimate generalization performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leave-one-out-cross-validation--287147f48ab24c4e))
- **leave-one-out-cross-validation**: Use when you must rigorously quantify tauFisher prediction accuracy on a small labeled time-series (e.g., GSE83855 time-restricted feeding schedules ZT5-ZT9 and ZT0-ZT4) without a separate test set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leave-one-out-cross-validation--7200d31ffe63aaa4))
- **leave-one-out-cross-validation**: Use when comparing cell-composition estimation methods (Bisque, CIBERSORT, BSEQ-sc, MuSiC, CIBERSORTx) on a cohort where a subset of individuals has both bulk RNA-seq and single-nucleus/single-cell data, to get global R and RMSD. - **leave-one-out-cross-validation-evaluation**: Use when a trained methylation typing model must be assessed for generalization to unseen motifs: hold out each of the 46 motifs (from 7 bacterial species) in turn, retrain on the remaining 45, and aggregate fold-level accuracies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/leave-one-out-cross-validation-evaluation))
- **leave-one-out-cross-validation-evaluation**: Use when a database-backed MS/MS identification method needs unbiased performance estimates on a closed compound set — e.g. 662 KEGG spectra scored by deep neural network structural similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/leave-one-out-cross-validation-evaluation))
- **leave-one-out-cross-validation-evaluation**: Use when you have a spatial dataset measuring a small panel of genes (e.g. bdtnp.txt in situ values at 3039 Drosophila embryo positions) and a matched scRNA-seq matrix, and you need to quantify how accurately an optimal-transport mapping reconstructs spatial expression of held-out genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leave-one-out-cross-validation-evaluation))
- **leave-one-out-cross-validation-of-calibration-models**: Use when you have calibration data on standard controls of known methylation percentage (e.g. 0–100% AMP dilutions) measured across multiple CpGs and need to compare predictive performance of competing calibration models per assay. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/leave-one-out-cross-validation-of-calibration-models))
- **leave-one-out-cross-validation-of-spatial-gene-expression**: Use when scRNA-seq data are being mapped onto a spatial reference map of a small number of measured genes (e.g. 64-position in situ hybridization maps) and an unbiased estimate of spatial gene-expression reconstruction quality is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leave-one-out-cross-validation-of-spatial-gene-expression))
- **leave-one-out-entropy-perturbation-ranking**: Use when a scRNA-Seq snapshot contains a multipotent population (e.g. MPPs) with competing downstream lineages, candidate priming RFs have already been filtered (type-1, low MPP-expression frequency), and you must prioritize which RFs and RF pairs define the primed state. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leave-one-out-entropy-perturbation-ranking))
- **leave-one-out-marker-gene-validation**: Use when an image-based ST panel omits genes that are measured in a reference or validation set, and you need to quantify imputation accuracy (MAE) against measured expression for held-out marker genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leave-one-out-marker-gene-validation))
- **leave-one-out-marker-subset-cross-validation**: Use when a MAPiT (or other measure-preserving pseudotime) reconstruction must be shown to be robust to the choice of markers fed into the pseudotime algorithm — e.g. spheroid flow cytometry data with multiple stains where input-marker bias is a concern. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leave-one-out-marker-subset-cross-validation))
- **leave-one-out-model-validation**: Use when validating a PROGENy model built from the 11-pathway perturbation compendium (208 ArrayExpress submissions, 580 experiments): you need evidence that pathway scores recover the perturbed pathway for experiments not seen during model construction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leave-one-out-model-validation))

- **lecture-plan**: 강의 기획서를 만든다. 제목·소개·대상·학습 목표·꼭지별 목차를 한 문서로 묶고 꼭지마다 시간과 산출물을 붙이며, 사전 질문지와 다음 단계 진입 체크리스트를 함께 낸다. 강의를 맡았는데 무엇을 몇 시간 동안 다룰지부터 정해야 할 때 고른다. 트리거: 강의안, 강의 기획서, 강의 제안서, 교육 계획서, 커리큘럼 설계, 강의 목차, 학습 목표, 출강 제안서. 자료 조사는 lecture-research, 슬라이드 설계는 lecture-blueprint를 쓴다. ([methylo/claude-skill-book](https://github.com/methylo/claude-skill-book/tree/HEAD/skills/04-lecture/lecture-plan) · ★12)
- **lecture-plan**: 강의, 워크숍 기획서. 대상, 학습 목표, 시간, 꼭지, 수강생 산출물 + 인계. 트리거: 강의 기획, 워크숍, 강의안, 커리큘럼, 학습 목표, 목차, 출강. 조사 lecture-research, 메시지 lecture-blueprint. ([ReinaMoon/writeskills-ko](https://github.com/ReinaMoon/writeskills-ko/tree/HEAD/skills/04-lecture/lecture-plan) · ★1)

"강의안 만들어줘", "클로드 레벨 3", "힉스필드 레벨2 강의안", "강의안 업데이트" 같은 요청에 사용. 생성 시점의 최신 공식 정보를 웹검색으로 팩트체크하고, 같은 툴의 이전 레벨 강의안과 이어지도록 만든다. ([growzero-cloud/my-first-git](https://github.com/growzero-cloud/my-first-git/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lecture-plan))
- **lecture-prep-notes**: Plans a lecture with learning outcomes, timed segments, examples, questions for the room, likely confusions, and a reflection to fill in afterward. Use when a professor, lecturer, or teaching assistant wants to prepare a lecture, plan a class session, turn slides or a reading into a teaching plan, or rework last year's lecture notes. ([phillips-jon/note-taking-skills](https://github.com/phillips-jon/note-taking-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/notes-by-interest/skills/lecture-prep-notes) · ★6)
- **lecture-reaction-sheet**: 大学の講義の感想シート（リアクションペーパー）作成を、学生が講義を十分理解し自分の言葉で書けるようになるまで対話的にガイドするスキル。社会学を学ぶ学部2〜3回生が対象。「感想シート」「リアクションペーパー」「講義の感想」「講義の感想を書きたい」と言われたとき、またはこのスキルが明示的に指定されたときに必ず使う。単に感想文を代筆・生成するのではなく、講義資料（必須）と音声/動画（任意）をもとに、選択式・記述式のクイズで理解度を完璧に確認し、学生自身の日常体験や他講義・社会学的概念との接続を問うてから、学生が自分の言葉で書いた感想を仕上げる。完成版を安易に渡さないことを最優先する。 ([nakane-labo/Skill](https://github.com/nakane-labo/Skill/tree/HEAD/lecture-reaction-sheet) · ★1)
- **lecture-recording-captions**: 밤밤의 강연·라이브·기수 세션 녹화본(줌 클라우드·디스코드·맥 화면기록)을 자막판 영상으로 만들고 배포할 때 읽을 것. 어절 하이라이트 자막, 슬라이드별 타이틀 카드, 핵심 영역 확대, 주아체 일러스트, 얼굴 PIP, 질의응답 웹캠 레이아웃, 엔드카드에서 유튜브 업로드·디스코드 공지·7일 만료까지 한 파이프라인. "녹화본 자막 달아줘", "강의 영상 편집해서 올려줘", "녹화본 디스코드에 업데이트해줘", "라이브 자막판 파이프라인" 요청을 처리한다. ([bam-bam-2/solo-skills](https://github.com/bam-bam-2/solo-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lecture-recording-captions) · ★448)

- **lecture-research**: 기획서의 꼭지마다 필요한 자료를 모아 강의용 노트로 정리한다. 도입 훅·핵심 개념·수치·사례·본인 사례·반박과 응답·실습 소재·구술 멘트까지 슬롯을 채우고, 자료마다 시각화 적합도와 만료일을 붙인다. 목차는 나왔는데 각 꼭지에서 할 말이 비어 있을 때 고른다. 트리거: 강의 리서치, 강의 자료 조사, 교안 리서치, 강의 사례 조사, 실습 소재, RL 노트, 데이터 포인트. 기획서는 lecture-plan, 일반 조사는 research-topic을 쓴다. - **lecture-review**: Use when 用户拿到讲课/培训/演讲/分享活动的 raw 转录稿（听悟/FunASR），要复盘主讲人的讲授表现——口癖、语言习惯、节奏、时间分配、承诺回收、互动密度；有定稿课件时可做 deck 逐页对照（哪页讲了/跳过/挪位、min/页超载预警）；用户显式要求"课程结构复盘"时进入高级模式——课件自动体检 + 课件×实讲双向比对，产出智合复盘式结构诊断与修改建议；或要跨场次对比讲师表现、复查上次的口癖改掉了没有。触发词：讲课复盘、讲课表现、口癖分析、节奏分析、讲师档案、课件对照、课程结构复盘、复盘课程设计。不要用于：纠正转录错字、改写生成课程内容；常规复盘不评课程内容质量与方法论对错（课程评价只在高级模式经显式触发后进行）。 ([cat-xierluo/legal-skills](https://github.com/cat-xierluo/legal-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lecture-review) · ★820)
- **lecture-review**: 완성된 강의 슬라이드를 읽고 고칠 곳을 짚어 반영한다. 구조 흐름·분량 배분·텍스트 품질·시각 자료 배치·발표 전환 논리를 나눠 보고, 설계 문서가 있으면 반영률까지 확인한다. 피드백 리포트를 먼저 내고 승인을 받은 뒤 수정한다. 덱은 다 만들었고 발표 전에 걸러야 할 때 고른다. 트리거: 슬라이드 리뷰, 슬라이드 점검, 강의 자료 피드백, pptx 리뷰, 슬라이드 퇴고, 발표 자료 점검, 슬라이드 보완. 슬라이드 생성은 lecture-slides를 쓴다. - **lecture-skill**: CS 课程系统性学习 Skill。融合横纵分析法与结构化写作质检体系，用于深度学习一门计算机科学课程（含 lecture notes、syllabus、paper list），最终输出一份结构完整、有个人洞察的学习报告。 触发词包括但不限于：学习这门课、帮我梳理一下这个课程、研究一下这门 CS 课程、输出学习报告、课程分析、lecture notes 整理、帮我搞懂这门课。 适用于用户丢来一个课程主页（如 https://cs.brown.edu/courses/csci1650/）说"帮我学习一下"或"帮我写个学习报告"的场景。 不要用于简单名词解释（如"什么是 OS"），也不要用于写公众号文章。 ([aresbit/MateBot2tg](https://github.com/aresbit/MateBot2tg/tree/HEAD/skills/lecture-skill) · ★45)

- **lecture-slides**: 강의 슬라이드(.pptx)를 만든다. 설계 문서(BP 노트)가 있으면 그대로 조판하고 없으면 주제·대상·시간을 받아 구조부터 잡으며, 도형과 텍스트로 시각 자료를 직접 구성하고 발표 노트까지 낸다. 내용이 정해져 실제 파일이 필요할 때 고른다. 트리거: 강의 슬라이드, 강의 자료, 교육 자료, 교안 작성, 워크숍 슬라이드, 발표 스크립트, 강사 노트. 슬라이드 설계는 lecture-blueprint, 완성본 점검은 lecture-review를 쓴다. - **lecture-slides**: 制作 HTML 课件 / Lecture Slides 的完整体系，风格唯一：04-magazine 杂志排版风（米色纸面、衬线大标题、圆角卡片 + 顶部强调线、嵌套去重），并含 Keynote 式演讲者模式（观众屏全屏 + 演讲者屏预览/文稿/双进度条，按讲稿语速估算实时对比讲课节奏，file:// 双击可用），字体/媒体全离线本地化（VF 子集化，任何电脑断网打开完美复现），以及质量门禁与进阶组件（媒体框宽高比审计、布局平衡/留白审计、多页递进图像素对齐、视频片段编辑器 start/end 持久化），和从 151 条真实排版反馈归纳出的默认排版规律（Layout Doctrine：媒体主角放大、卡片等比不裁剪、砌砖无缝铺满、文字窄列让位、中英对照体系、渐进揭示动画）。当用户提到"做课件""lecture slides""课件样式/风格""框体样式统一""嵌套边框重复""演讲者视图""演讲者模式""双屏放映""presenter view""双进度条""讲课节奏""杂志风/编辑风课件""字体本地化""离线打开""自包含课件""排版优化""内容堆顶部/留白""媒体框比例/宽高比""递进图对齐""视频片段/剪辑区间""排版规律""布局偏好""媒体放大/铺满""砌砖布局""图文混排""卡片编辑器""改卡片/移动卡片""删卡片""缩放卡片""替换媒体""裁剪媒体""四角控件""编辑课件… ([YuliangXiu/lecture-slides](https://github.com/YuliangXiu/lecture-slides/tree/HEAD/lecture-slides) · ★12)
